## 1. Family / fuzz-0 status
Files: `pi-ai/src/providers/openai-codex-responses.ts`, `pi-ai/src/types.ts`, `pi-ai/dist/types/types.d.ts` (matches manifest `paths`, manifest:319-331).
18.16.0 patch = 12 hunks / 3 files. Replay on 18.18.0 (`~/.cache/omp-plan-18180/replay-logs/0069-...txt`): **0 FAILED hunks**; 10 hunks applied with offset, 2 exactly.
Offsets: codex hunks −1/−32/−124/−113; both `types` hunks +10. Cause of the drift: 18.17.0 moved Codex WS plumbing out of this file (`isCodexStreamProgressEvent`, `extractCodexFrame*`, `notifyCodexWebSocket*`, `toCodexHeaders`, `toWebSocketUrl` are gone from 18.18.0's function list; `openCodexWebSocketTransport` 18.16:1789 → 18.18:1665). All patch anchors still resolve: `applyCodexStableEffort` 18.18:1439, `buildTransformedCodexRequestBody` 18.18:1377, `buildCodexRequestContext` 18.18:1346, `finalize()` payload write 18.18:3162.

## 2. Per-hunk diagnosis
No failing hunks. Two hunk families need eyeballing only:
- Hunk 2 deletes the upstream doc comment `/** configuration_update effort baselines, keyed by model + session. */` above `effortControls` (present in pristine 18.18.0 at openai-codex-responses.ts:451). Pure cosmetic deletion inherited from the old base — **restore it in the rebased patch** (keep the patch additive).
- Hunk 4 inserts a stray blank line before `isCodexWebSocketRetryableStreamError` (18.18:1007). Drop the blank line; otherwise the patch keeps accreting whitespace-only deltas.
No upstream commit rewrote the function under the patch: `applyCodexStableEffort` in 18.18.0 (1439-1461) is byte-for-byte the 18.16.0 pre-patch body (18.16:1563-1585).

## 3. Classification
**Class: KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL (applies as-is; one gate edit + 3 semantic confirmations).** Not ADOPT-CANDIDATE: 18.18.0 still has **no** durable effort baseline. Grep for `effortControl` in 18.18.0 `pi-ai/src` returns only the in-memory `Map` (openai-codex-responses.ts:452, openai-codex-state.ts:47,60,69) and the planner (openai-configuration-update.ts:40-96,161+); nothing writes it into `providerPayload` and nothing reads it back from history. Upstream resume = `resolveOpenAIEffortControlState` parent copy only (openai-configuration-update.ts:85-96), which the patch deliberately preserves via its `hasParentState` branch.

Semantic confirmations the rebase must re-assert (each is a gate leg to run):
1. `getOpenAIResponsesHistoryPayload` spreads the payload (18.18.0 utils.ts:691) → `effortControl` survives load/replay and is **not** turned into a wire item (`getOpenAIResponsesHistoryItems` reads `.items` only, utils.ts:694-700).
2. Remote-compaction payload rebuild (pi-coding-agent `src/session/session-context.ts:220-231`) constructs `{type, provider, items}` with no `effortControl` → the patch's "legacy latest response starts fresh, never revive an older snapshot" rule fires; intended, already asserted by the `compacted` case.
3. `normalizeSessionMessageForProviderReplay` drops `providerPayload` for responses-family assistants (pi-coding-agent `src/session/messages.ts:334-366`, identical in 18.16.0:334-366) → the new field cannot perturb provider-replay equality or cache-prefix comparisons.

Rebase method (3 steps): (a) regenerate the patch against 18.18.0 PRistine as a pure addition (restore the upstream comment, drop the stray blank line); (b) add `"18.18.0"` to the gate's three version lists — `patches/verify-codex-effort-resume.ts:28` (allow-list, currently fails closed with `unsupported Codex effort resume gate version`), `:38` and `:142` (18.10+ branch that expects `initialEffort = "low"` and the persisted-snapshot assertion); (c) no source edits — the 18.18.0 code paths the patch wraps are unchanged, and `gpt-6-astra` still resolves `supportsConfigurationUpdate` (pi-catalog `compat/rules/classes/openai.kdl:65-66`, revision 6.0.0) and supports `low` (`openai.kdl:43-44`), so the fixture's expectations hold unchanged.
Owning gate: `0069 verify-codex-effort-resume.ts` (patches/apply.sh:532). Effort: **S** (~0.5-1h: version-list bump, patch regeneration, gate run).

## 4. Upstream changes near the owned paths that could break the invariant
1. **18.17.0 Codex WS refactor / incremental input fix** ("Fixed Codex WebSocket turns sending malformed incremental input after reconnects or onPayload edits", #17809) + shared `buildResponsesDeltaInput` (openai-shared.ts:4445-4485) mean the WS delta is a *strict* item prefix. The patch inserts `configuration_update` at a non-tail index (`openai-configuration-update.ts:178-207`), so `deepEqualsWithout(previous, current, …)` fails on the turn an effort actually changes → full-context replay once, then the chain resumes. Severity **medium, cost-only (no correctness/prefix corruption)**; unchanged from 18.16.0, but now shared with standard Responses. Gate leg: the resume leg already compares full prefixes (`prefix(warm)` vs `prefix(resumed)`); add an explicit assertion that `updates(wire)` stays `[{…xhigh}]` across a chained second turn.
2. **18.17.0 continuation after an output-limit cut tool call** (#18189, openai-responses + Codex): a continuation replay drops/reorders input items. The patch fails safe here — `syncOpenAIEffortControlState` resets on any index/anchor mismatch (openai-configuration-update.ts:141-147), so the worst case is a fresh baseline, never a 400 or a stale `configuration_update`. Severity low.
3. **18.17.0 standard Responses WebSockets / `statefulResponses`**: separate provider with its own `effortControls` map (openai-responses.ts:451) — 0069 does not touch it; no cross-talk. Severity none.
4. **18.17.0 stopped sending `OpenAI-Beta: responses=experimental`** on Codex HTTP: headers only, no body/cache effect. Severity none.
5. **Cache-prefix invariant**: the patch keeps top-level `reasoning.effort` pinned to `baseEffort` and moves changes into `input`, so `reasoning` never becomes a per-turn byte difference; persistence itself is off-wire (gate asserts `!("effortControl" in wire)`). Nothing in 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes `transformRequestBody` effort mapping (openai-codex/request-transformer.ts:164-172) or the codex catalog effort ladder (openai-codex.kdl:165-167 identical in both trees). Severity none.
6. **OAuth / auth**: no owned path; the 18.17.0 `AuthStorage`-over-backend change belongs to family-usage-pipeline/0090. Severity none for 0069.

## 5. Worker-thread assumption (task.workerThreads, default 4)
**No — the capability does not rely on process-global or main-thread-only state.** Evidence:
- Effort state lives in the `ProviderSessionState` map owned by each `AgentSession` (pi-coding-agent `src/session/agent-session.ts:1442`, passed at :2800/:2867), and a subagent running on a worker constructs its *own* `AgentSession` through the same `createAgentSession` path (`src/thread/worker-warm-up.ts:47`; worker-homed sessions are published/mirrored, not shared: `src/thread/session-directory.ts:44-59`). Each thread therefore gets an independent `effortControls` map by construction — including on the main thread.
- The only cross-thread carrier is the durable snapshot inside `providerPayload`, which survives the session file and the RPC mirror (`src/rpc/wire/content.ts:121` declares `providerPayload?: Record<string, unknown>`; nothing in `src/thread/**` strips it — grep returns no matches).
- A worker-thread subagent whose `parentSessionId` names a main-thread session simply finds no local parent state (`hasParentState === false`) and restores from its own history, which is the intended path; it does not need the parent's map.
Failure mode *if* this assumption ever broke: no crash — silent baseline drift, i.e. a per-turn `reasoning.effort` change against a server that already holds a `configuration_update` chain ⇒ prompt-cache miss on every turn (repeated full-price input) and possibly a `configuration_update`-adjacent rejection. Cache/cost loss, not a crash.
Gate coverage today: `closeStates()` + fresh `Map` (gate:177-181 `warm` vs `resumed`) already simulates the "separate module singleton / separate thread" case and asserts byte-identical prefixes. **Missing leg to add:** call `buildTransformedCodexRequestBody` with `providerSessionState: undefined` (the patch's second branch, openai-codex-responses.ts patched `applyCodexStableEffort`) and assert no throw, `effort === baseEffort` from history, and `updates()` preserved — that is precisely the worker-thread shape where no map is handed in. Without that leg, a regression in the `createOpenAIEffortControlState` fallback would only surface as silent spend.

### 3. Per capability
**0090 — class: KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL (2 hunks) + one mandatory CONFLICT-REDESIGN add-on in upstream-new files.**

(a) Mechanical rebase of the 2 hunks (no semantic loss):
- Hunk 3: keep upstream's `{models, error}` contract and only widen the parameter: `fetcher: (previous?: readonly Model<TApi>[]) => Promise<readonly ModelSpec<TApi>[] | null>` with `await fetcher(previous)` inside the existing `try` (18.18.0 `model-manager.ts:534-543`).

- Post-rebase the retention really works because `google.ts`/`special.ts` hunks applied (`fetchDynamicModels: async previous => …`, patch lines 2979+, 3086+).

(b) CONFLICT-REDESIGN (upstream-new worker bridge; not a fuzz failure — a *missing-file* failure):
- Our patch adds two **required** members to `OAuthApi` (`patch:404,414` in `dist/types/auth/types.d.ts`; `patch:2582,2592` in `src/auth/types.ts`; impl `patch:609-615` in `src/auth/oauth.ts`): `inspectUnresolvedRefresh`, `unlockAndRefreshStored`.
- 18.18.0 adds `class RemoteOAuth implements OAuthApi` (`pi-coding-agent/src/thread/services/auth-remote.ts:341-405`) with neither member → `tsc` "incorrectly implements interface" on the patched tree (nothing in `patches/` typechecks today), and at runtime `authStorage.oauth.inspectUnresolvedRefresh is not a function` in a worker thread.

Both APIs take/return plain data (fence string, verdict record) so a worker forward is legitimate — unlike `refreshStored`, which upstream refuses on workers (`auth-remote.ts:398-405`) because it takes sync hooks.

Gates (all 5 registered for 0090 at `patches/apply.sh:564,569,572,578-579`, plus a new one):
- `verify-oauth-durable-refresh.ts` (token-loss/replay/fence semantics), `verify-oauth-unlock-fence-faults.sh` (fence mutation + missing-store-member fail-closed), `verify-auth-unlock-extension.ts` (`/auth-unlock` routing contract), `verify-codex-account-eligibility-retention.ts`, `verify-google-account-eligibility-retention.ts`.
- The two retention gates call the real `resolveProviderModels` (`verify-google-…ts:46-47`, phases 2/3 at `:163-199`; `verify-codex-…ts:34-35`, `:108-122`), so the "previous never passed" state goes **red**, not silent — this is the required gate leg for the mechanical part.
- **New**: `verify-worker-auth-bridge.ts` (absent today — `grep workerThreads|SubagentWorker` over `patches/` → no matches).

Effort: **M** (2 small hunks + 3-file bridge + 1 new gate).

### 4. Upstream 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes near the owned paths (invariant risk)
- `pi-ai/src/auth/*`: byte-size-identical for `refresh.ts` (50.6KB), `store.ts` (12.1KB), `sqlite-credential-store.ts` (80.5KB), `types.ts` (59.1KB), `usage.ts` (35.6KB), `health.ts`, `pool.ts`; signatures confirmed unchanged (`store.ts:59-65` still requires `tryDisableAuthCredentialIfMatches`; `types.ts:376-383` override still `Promise<OAuthCredentials>`; `refresh.ts:48` cooldown, `:147-155` in-memory maps, `:281-283` `#persistExchangedCredential`). **No OAuth-invariant regression from upstream.** `oauth.ts` 14.5→14.6KB and `select.ts` 49.5→49.6KB / 1252→1249 lines differ only in text; member lists and the `tryOAuth`/preflight bodies compared line-by-line are identical (`select.ts:1128-1170` both trees) — doc/comment churn, no behaviour change. **No cache-prefix or compaction path is touched by this family.**
- `pi-catalog/src/model-manager.ts`: the only real upstream change (dynamic-fetch error capture, above). Silent-breakage vector: the applied `previous?:` signature makes the retention a no-op without a compile/runtime error. Gate: the two retention gates (they exercise `resolveProviderModels`, so they fail red).
- `pi-coding-agent/src/thread/services/*` (new in 18.17/18.18): collides with the `OAuthApi` extension (section 3b). Silent-breakage vector: if no typecheck runs in the build, the missing methods surface only as a TypeError on a "no API key" subagent turn.
- `pi-ai/src/registry/oauth/anthropic.ts`, `engine/{common,refresh}.ts`: hunks apply; no upstream edits in the patched regions (the `anthropic-identity` refresh-phase change and the `oauthError` field still land on 18.18.0 context).
- Adjacent (not this family's gate): Codex/Antigravity account retention is also fed by `special.ts:70`/`google.ts:108`; if a later family rewrites those fetch closures, keep the `previous` parameter or the retention dies quietly.

### 5. Worker-thread assumption (workerThreads>0)
**YES — the capability has a worker-thread assumption, but only at its session/extension surface, and today it fails hard (type + runtime), not silently.**
- Auth state is *not* thread-local: a worker's `AuthStorage` is built over `RemoteAuthStorageBackend` and every namespace call runs on main (`pi-coding-agent/src/thread/services/auth.ts:1-20` doc, `:490-492` `new AuthStorage(new RemoteAuthStorageBackend(remote))`; `auth-remote.ts:633-637`). `refreshStored` on a worker is explicitly refused (`auth-remote.ts:398-405`). Therefore the durable exchange journal, `#oauthRefreshInFlight` (`refresh.ts:147`), `#recentMints` (`:150`), `#stalledRefreshes` (`:155`) and the lease/CAS logic all keep a single owner (main): no double POST, no cache/prefix involvement, no SQLite cross-thread contention from subagents.
- Concrete failure with `workerThreads>0` (now default 4, `pi-coding-agent/src/task/settings.ts:438`; workers join at ≥9 concurrent subagents, `subagent-worker.ts:78`): `agent-session` runs inside workers for subagent sessions, and the patched preflight calls `authStorage.oauth.inspectUnresolvedRefresh(...)` **unconditionally** in the `!apiKey` branch (`patch:3189` and `patch:3204`; 18.18.0 source anchor `agent-session.ts:9177`). `RemoteOAuth` has no such method → `TypeError`, so a subagent turn that would fail with the useful `PromptPreflightError` ("No API key found…") instead dies with an internal error. The `/auth-unlock` extension routed through a worker `AuthStorage` would throw the same way. This is a **crash**, not silent cost/cache loss.
- The durable-refresh core itself has **no** worker assumption once the bridge exists: `unlockAndRefreshStored` and `inspectUnresolvedRefresh` are plain-data calls and are forwarded to main, where the fence/journal/lease code runs against the one store (worker-visible reads fail closed with `reason: "not-applicable"` for providers without a journal, patch `:893-896`).
- Gate legs needed (none exist today):
  1. **Compile leg**: run `tsc --noEmit` (or the release declaration build) over the patched tree so `class RemoteOAuth implements OAuthApi` fails closed instead of deferring to runtime.
  2. **New `verify-worker-auth-bridge.ts`**: instantiate/export `SubagentWorker` (threshold const is 9, `subagent-worker.ts:78` — drive the class directly instead of spawning 9 subagents) with `task.workerThreads>0`; assert (i) a worker subagent prompt with no API key throws `PromptPreflightError` with the main-thread message, not `TypeError`; (ii) `inspectUnresolvedRefresh` over RPC returns `eligible:false` + `reason:"not-applicable"` for a non-journal provider and the real pending fence for anthropic after a fault-injected pending exchange; (iii) `unlockAndRefreshStored` invoked from the worker performs exactly **one** token POST (count `globalThis.fetch`), persists the row, and the main-thread store's journal row is discharged — this is the leg that catches "worker mints a second token" if anyone later moves the exchange off main; (iv) `oauth.refreshStored` from a worker still throws the upstream refusal (keeps the single-owner invariant explicit).
  3. Keep `verify-auth-unlock-extension.ts` green unchanged (it drives the extension on main; the worker case is the new gate).

Out-of-scope confirmation: no file under `~/.omp`, `~/.bun`, `agent.db`/`models.db`, credentials or caches was read or written; only the pristine trees under `/home/chris/.cache/omp-plan-18180/` and the repo's `patches/` were inspected, and no command was executed.

### 5. Worker-thread assumption (workerThreads > 0)

- **0037: no.** State is per-`StatusLineComponent` instance (`component.ts:852-856`), not a module singleton; the TUI renderer exists only on the main thread, and worker-hosted subagents never construct one. No cross-thread shared map, timer, or auth store is involved.

- **0030: no.** Cache keys are pure functions of `(provider, baseUrl, credential identity)`; with one shared `AuthStorage` the canonical key makes worker-and-main writes converge. Failure mode if the sharing were ever dropped back to per-thread stores: the exact pre-0030 read/write split — stale quota rendered as "fetched 1d ago", **silent**, no crash.
- **0031: YES.** The provider restriction travels as an in-band function argument, and the 18.18.0 thread boundary only carries `provider` — so `fetchUsageReports(signal)` arriving from `session-publisher.ts:623` / `remote-agent-session.ts:649` / `agent-session-handle.ts:351`, and any worker-side `usage.reports({providers})` through `auth-remote.ts:473-483`, degrade to an **unfiltered aggregate fan-out**. Concrete failure mode with `workerThreads=4`: a worker/remote client asking about one provider polls every configured provider, including Anthropic `/api/oauth/usage` — exactly the per-IP rate-limited endpoint 0037 gates — and the aggregate result lands in a different broker scope (`AGGREGATE_USAGE_SCOPE`, `remote-store.ts:124-128` patched) than the statusline's provider-scoped entry, so the 429 never surfaces as an error; the statusline keeps rendering `lastGood` and the extra cost is invisible. Crash: none. **Gate leg that would catch it:** (a) static — `WireUsageReportsOptions` must carry `providers` and `session-publisher.fetchUsageReports`/`agent-session-handle`/`remote-agent-session` must forward the provider argument; (b) behaviour — drive a mocked `RemoteAuthStorageBackend` with `reports({providers:[p]})` and assert main receives the set and probes exactly one provider; (c) 0037 behaviour gate: slow-mock `fetchUsageReports` and count calls (idle repaint = 0 for anthropic, turn boundary ≤ 1/60 s, provider switch = 1, re-entry = 1) — none of the three exists today, so the upgrade must add one gate script (`verify-usage-statusline-gate.ts`) rather than only rebasing the patch.

### Plan-level asks for the main agent
1. 0025 drop is a 5-edit mechanical change (patch block, manifest path, manifest sha256, `apply.sh:497`, delete verify script) and touches no other family; 0090 does *not* depend on it.
2. Do **not** touch 0026/0030: `0090-omp-oauth-durable-refresh` hunks are written against `invalidateReport`/`readReport` and will fail if those helpers change name or shape.
3. Add the missing behaviour gate for 0037 (throttle + provider scoping) and the thread-boundary legs above; that gate is the acceptance instrument for "workerThreads=4, all gates PASS".
4. No verification of this family was executed by me beyond reading the recorded fuzz-0 replay and the pristine 18.18.0 sources (read-only; no runtime, DB, credential or cache access).

- Capability: idle warming horizon is an extension-owned process-local budget (`PI_CACHE_WARMING_IDLE_MAX_MS`), a run that hits the horizon is **parked** with its request and resumes from the entry's own clock, and each refresh that reached the provider is reported as `cache_warming_result`.

|---|---|---|

| `dist/types/session/cache-warmer.d.ts` #4 | `get settling(): boolean` after constructor | upstream inserted `get confirmedView()` (`cache-warmer.ts:406`) and `stopsAt`/`sentAt` in `CacheWarmingStatus` (`cache-warmer.ts:246-259`), so the constructor→`get status` context moved. |
| `src/session/cache-warmer.ts` #6 | `defer`/`report` deps + class doc | `CacheWarmerDeps` (18.18 `cache-warmer.ts:350-367`) is unchanged in shape but the class doc now describes dispatch-anchored horizons (`:369-373`). |

**B. Upstream rewrote the logic the hunk intersects (9 hunks, semantic)**
- #5 `@@-262` (`PARKED_POLL_MS` + `ActiveRun.touchedAt`): `ActiveRun` changed — `isCurrent: () => boolean` replaced by `change: () => PromptCacheChange | undefined`, plus `refreshSentAt`/`warmingStopReason`/`entry` (18.18 `cache-warmer.ts:274-294`). Probable commit: the `cache` status-line segment (#17496, `CHANGELOG.md:104`) [INFERENCE — no shell access for `git log`; bare repo not readable as text]. **Consequence: `dispatched()` (18.18 `:475-529`) restarts the entry clock when the provider actually sends, and `#touch` (`:605-610`) is the only writer of `entry.touchedAt`. Our `run.touchedAt` field is now a duplicate of `entry.touchedAt`.**
- #7 `@@-339` (`#settling`, `get settling`, `get status`): `status` became `#statusOf(change)` + `confirmedView` (`:398-436`).
- #10 `@@-482` (`#idleStopReason` label + `#report`): neighbours changed — `#touch` (`:605`) now sits between `#idleStopReason` and `#clearRun`, and `#idleStopReason` is still the stock 30-min constant (`:595-602`).
- #11 `@@-498` **applied but is now wrong**: it replaces `#schedule` with the `touchedAt`-anchored version. `run.touchedAt` was never added (#5 failed), and `#resumeParked` calls `parked.isCurrent()` which no longer exists → runtime `TypeError` inside a `setTimeout` (Bun: process crash), and `base = run.touchedAt ?? Date.now()` silently degrades to raise-time anchoring (refresh scheduled past expiry → paid miss). **Partial application is worse than a clean failure; rebase these hunks as one unit and never ship a tree where `#schedule` references `touchedAt`.**
- #12/#13 `@@-551`/`@@-565` (defer gates + settling ownership around `#refresh`): `#refresh` was rewritten (`cache-warmer.ts:670-740`): `run.refreshSentAt = undefined` on entry, `queuedAt`, `sentAt = run.refreshSentAt ?? queuedAt`, `onWarmed(message, override, refreshOutcome)` now takes a third argument, and a miss with `cacheWrite > 0` calls `#touch` before stopping (`:709-717`).
- #15/#16 `@@-596`/`@@-605` (`#report("miss")`, `#report("hit")` + `run.touchedAt = undefined`): same rewritten tail of `#refresh`.

**C. Collateral from a dependency family (idle-maintenance #6/#11/#13)**
- #6 edits `#isQuiet` whose hunk context includes `!session.hasIdleEphemeralReservation` — that line comes from family **0010**, whose own `idle-maintenance.ts` hunk #3 FAILED on 18.18.0 (`replay-logs/0010-...txt`). 0092's idle-owner design also rides 0010's `IdleMaintenance.isRecapRunning` and `AgentSession.hasIdleEphemeralReservation` (`0010` patch lines 63, 829, 1005). **Rebase order: 0010 first, then 0092.**
- #11/#13 (`#onRecapDue`, `#runRecap`) fail because upstream replaced the recap gate: 18.18 uses `#isSettled()` instead of `#isQuiet()` (`idle-maintenance.ts:318`, `:349`) and arms *both* timers only after `waitForSessionSettlement` (`:219-227`, changed from 18.16 `:189-196` which armed the recap before).

## 3. Capability classification
| cap | class | rebase method | gate(s) | effort |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0092 | **KEEP-REBASE-SEMANTIC** | (a) drop `ActiveRun.touchedAt`; anchor every schedule/park/resume on `run.entry.touchedAt` and reuse `#delayFor(model, entry.tier)` instead of a local `promptCache[tier]` lookup; (b) `isCurrent()` → `change()` in `#park` copy, `#resumeParked`, and `#idleStopReason` callers; (c) `dispatched()` from `start`/`markPromptCacheDispatched` already reschedules, so park/poll must not fight it — `#park` copies the run and keeps `entry` by reference; (d) put `#settling += 1` around `onRefreshStart`→stream settle inside 18.18's try/finally and keep `report` next to the new `#touch` miss path; (e) `stopsAt` (`cache-warmer.ts:433`) must use `idleWarmingAgeMs()` or the `cache` segment lies (`prompt-cache-status.ts:61`); (f) idle-maintenance: keep the replay out of settlement (see §4.6) and re-arm the fired step only. | `patches/verify-cache-warming-idle-budget.ts` (`apply.sh:581`), `patches/verify-cron-idle-owners.ts` (`apply.sh:585`), `patches/verify-session-cache-keepalive.ts` (`apply.sh:586`) | **L** |

Upstream did **not** adopt this capability: `MAX_IDLE_WARMING_AGE_MS` is still 30 min and unconditional (`cache-warmer.ts:61`, `:595-602`), and there is no result event. No ADOPT-CANDIDATE. No CONFLICT: nothing upstream forbids the env budget or parking.

**Gates need work, not just passing:** `verify-cache-warming-idle-budget.ts` calls `w.start({...}, () => true)` — 18.18 interprets the second arg as `change()` where a truthy value means "context changed", so the gate must pass `() => undefined` and fire the returned `dispatched()` closure; `apply.sh:584-587` registers the cron/keepalive gates **only when `OMP_PATCH_VERSION == 18.16.0`**, so on 18.18.0 they silently disappear unless re-pointed (acceptance criterion 2: retired gates gone, live gates present).

## 4. Upstream 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes near the owned paths that can break the invariant even when hunks apply
1. **Entry lifetime anchored at dispatch**, not at `start`: `start` returns `dispatched()` (`cache-warmer.ts:475-529`), session stores it per context (`agent-session.ts:6048-6055`), stream `onPayload` fires it (`sdk.ts:4900-4903`). Severity **high** (our park/resume anchoring silently wrong → full-prefix re-write).
2. **`isCurrent` → `change()`** (`cache-warmer.ts:275`, `:470-473`, `:479`, `:683`). Severity **high** (timer `TypeError` = crash, or silently "context changed" stop).
3. **`refreshSentAt` / `queuedAt` / `sentAt`** (`:289`, `:672`, `:700-701`, `:764-766`): "refreshing" now means dispatched. Our `settling` counter spans from before dispatch — deliberate, but see 6.
4. **Miss path keeps the entry**: `#touch` on `cacheWrite > 0` then stop (`:709-717`). Our `report("miss")` + stop must not also clear the parked anchor. Severity medium.
5. **`stopsAt` in the public status** (`:433` → `prompt-cache-status.ts:61`) bypasses the env horizon. Severity medium (observability/UI only, no cost).
6. **Idle recap re-gated on settlement and armed after it** (`idle-maintenance.ts:219-227`, `:318`, `:349`). If a warm replay is folded into `#isSettled`/`waitForSessionSettlement`, `:225` returns false and **nothing is armed** — the recap and idle compaction starve for the whole warmed/parked window (up to the entry TTL). Severity **high** (silent feature loss, no crash). Keep the replay in `#isQuiet` + the per-step `BUSY_RETRY_MS` retry instead.
7. **`runIdleCompaction` now cancels the warmer** when not streaming/compacting (`agent-session.ts:7647`) — `cancel()` must drop `#parked` (ours does via `#stop`→`#clearRun`→`#dropParked`; assert it in the gate).
8. **Compaction lead**: idle compaction still fires 5 s before the warmer's refresh point (`idle-maintenance.ts:263-277`). A parked run that resumes late makes the summary read a cold prefix. Severity medium; covered by `verify-cron-idle-owners.ts`.
9. `CacheWarmingResultEvent` must join the `ExtensionEvent` union and `ExtensionContext.on` overload (`src/extensibility/extensions/types.ts:1696`, `:1903` in 18.16 numbering; re-locate in 18.18). Mechanical.

## 5. Worker-thread assumption (task.workerThreads, default 4)
**No — 0092 does not assume main-thread-only state that worker threads would break, and the warmer never runs off the main thread**, by construction:
- Every spawned/subagent session opts out: `task/executor.ts:4944-4945` ("Subagents are short-lived; never schedule background warm requests. `cacheWarming: false`"), also `thread/worker-warm-up.ts:62`, `compress/session.ts:47`, `commit/agentic/agent.ts:65`. With `cacheWarming:false` no `CacheWarmer` is constructed (`sdk.ts:4808-4811`) → no parking, no `#settling`, no `cache_warming_result` emission in a worker.
- Everything 0092 adds is per-instance state: `#settling`, `#parked` (`cache-warmer.ts`), `#recapSettling` (`idle-maintenance.ts`), and the `AgentSession` getters `idleRecapRunning`/`cacheWarmReplaying`. No module-level map, no session registry, no auth store, no extension runner beyond the existing `extensionRunner.emit` the session already owns (`sdk.ts:4817-4818`).
- The one genuine process-global is `process.env.PI_CACHE_WARMING_IDLE_MAX_MS` (`cache-warmer.ts` const + `idleWarmingAgeMs()`, 18.18 target `:62-80`). Worker threads get their own `process.env` copy unless `SHARE_ENV` (Node semantics; Bun parity not verified here) [INFERENCE], so if a warmer were ever constructed in a worker it would read the 30-minute default and **silently not keep alive** (cost, not crash) while the cron extension's `/cron keep` reports success. That is the failure mode to guard, and today it is unreachable because of `cacheWarming:false`.
- **Gate legs to add** (none exist today — `verify-cache-warming-idle-budget.ts` is single-threaded and would not catch a cross-thread divergence): (i) assert a task-executor-spawned session has `cacheWarmer === undefined` while `task.workerThreads` is 4, (ii) drive the warmer in-process while a `Bun.Worker` subagent is alive and assert the parked run still hits its 54-minute refresh (no cancel, no missed arm), (iii) assert `idleWarmingAgeMs()` in a spawned worker and in the main thread agree, so the env dependency is proven inert or replaced by an injected `deps.getIdleHorizonMs` (preferred long-term fix). `verify-cron-idle-owners.ts` already pins "the host warmer defers to an in-flight idle recap" and is the natural home for (ii).

- fuzz-0 on 18.18.0: **1 hunk FAILS** (`pi-catalog/src/compat/rules.json`); the other 10 sections apply (`replay-logs/family-image-budget-snapcompact.txt`). Per main's correction the family total is 1, not 2.
- Applied cleanly and verified by reading the 18.18.0 trees: `snapcompact/dist/types/snapcompact.d.ts:266-271`, `snapcompact/src/snapcompact.ts:256-303`, `pi-catalog/src/compat/request-size.ts:48-60`, `pi-catalog/dist/types/compat/request-size.d.ts:17-23`, `providers/commandcode.kdl:10`, `opencode-go.kdl:3`, `zhipu-coding-plan.kdl:3`, `devin.kdl:3`.

## 2. The failing hunk
| Hunk | Cause | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| `rules.json @@ -1 +1 @@` (whole-file, one line) | **context drift on a generated artifact**: the `-` line is the 18.16.0-compiled JSON; 18.18.0 recompiled it after new KDL sources/rules (e.g. 18.17.0 `tool-name-prefix`, `supports-message-threads`; `pi-catalog/CHANGELOG.md:17-30`). Textual re-anchor is wrong by construction. | `pi-catalog/README.md:22-31` ("Never edit … rules.json by hand … `bun run gen:compat`"); `compat/types.ts:691-692` (`files` = sorted provenance list); `verify-catalog-regen-parity.ts:1-35` (`rules-files` claim: `rules.files` must equal the KDL tree on disk) |

Not an upstream adoption: 18.18.0 sets `image-count-budget` only on anthropic/bedrock/cursor/google*/openai*/openrouter/umans/xai/deepseek (`rules/providers/*.kdl`), never as a root `on-api` fallback, and never on commandcode/opencode-go/zhipu-coding-plan/devin.

## 3. Per capability
| Cap | What the patch adds on 18.16.0 | Class | Rebase method | Gate(s) | Effort |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0036 | `image-count-budget 200` in `opencode-go.kdl:3` and `zhipu-coding-plan.kdl:3` provider blocks | KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL (0 hunks fail) | apply verbatim; recompile `rules.json` (§ below) | `verify-provider-image-budget.ts` via `apply.sh:495` (`verifyCatalogBudgets`, `verifyClampBehaviour`) | S |
| 0044 | root `on-api` fallback file `providers/image-budget.kdl` (90 Anthropic/Bedrock, 200 everything else) + doc-comment only edits to `request-size.ts` / its `.d.ts` | KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL (new-file hunk applies; grammar still exists: `rules/README.md:155,187,201`, `cascade.ts:127`; `priority=-1` is still below deployment policy) | apply verbatim; the file must enter `rules.files` in sorted position between `huggingface.kdl` and `image-tokenization.kdl` | `verify-provider-image-budget.ts:432-493` (`verifyKdlBudgetRules`: KDL regex + compiled `source` provenance + resolver) | S |
| 0052 | `MODEL_FRAME_TOKEN_STEPS`/`measuredSteps` in `snapcompact.ts:256-303` + Command-Code account-alias rescue in `request-size.ts:49-59` | KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL (both hunks apply; upstream never adopted a measured-frame table — `frameBilling` at `snapcompact.ts:262-276` still returns only `rule`/`imageDetail`) | apply verbatim; nothing to regenerate beyond rules.json | `verify-snapcompact-model-billing.ts` via `apply.sh:496` (1568→995, 2576→1011, off-curve ids, alias api, `frameBillingKey` invalidation) | S |
| 0057 | `image-count-budget 200` in `devin.kdl:3` | KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL (0 hunks fail) | apply verbatim | `verify-provider-image-budget.ts:534-...` devin pair + `verifyCatalogBudgets:221` | S |

No ADOPT-CANDIDATE and no RETIRED-USER item in this family. No CONFLICT-REDESIGN: every semantic hunk applies and its targets are byte-identical where it matters (`request-size.ts` 18.16.0 ≡ 18.18.0, `frameBilling`/`frameTokens`/`frameBillingKey` and `FRAME_TOKEN_ESTIMATE=5024` unchanged at `snapcompact.ts:256-276,516`).

**rules.json rebase (the only real work):** the tarball ships no `scripts/` (`verify-catalog-regen-parity.ts:16-21`), so run the documented equivalent generator (the 0065 precedent, `.trellis/tasks/09-27-omp-1833-deploy/research/gen65.py:1-20`): start from 18.18.0's pristine `rules.json`, assert it round-trips byte-identically through `json.dumps(separators=(",",":"), ensure_ascii=False)`, re-apply our 5 KDL edits, insert the new rule entries + shift every `source` line number that follows, insert `providers/image-budget.kdl` into `files`, then assert the delta is exactly: one `files` entry, 2 root `on-api` rules (priority −1, budgets 90/200), 4 provider rules (+`imageCountBudget:200`), and shifted `source` refs only. Then `verify-catalog-regen-parity.ts` (`apply.sh:617`) + `verify-provider-image-budget.ts:452-474` prove the artifact is not stale.
Verification gates also need the new version arm: `verify-provider-image-budget.ts:39-42` and `verify-snapcompact-model-billing.ts:48-58` whitelist ends at `"18.16.0"` and fail closed otherwise; add `"18.18.0"`.

## 4. Upstream 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes near the owned paths
1. **snapcompact 18.17.0** — `compact()` now keeps image content from pre-16.1.0 archived histories (`snapcompact/CHANGELOG.md:5-9`). Touches what a dropped-history summary carries, i.e. the input to `plannedFrameTokens`/frame accounting, not the price functions. Severity **low**; owner: `verify-snapcompact-frame-reprice.ts` (0062) and `verify-snapcompact-model-billing.ts` (0052). Our measured table changes absolute prices only, so a changed frame count cannot silently restore the 5024 ceiling overcharge.
2. **pi-catalog 18.17.0** — new compat axes `tool-name-prefix`, `supports-message-threads` and opt-in Responses WebSocket (`CHANGELOG.md:17-30`). They recompile `rules.json` (the hunk failure) but do not touch `image-count-budget`/`image-max-edge` resolution. Severity **none for semantics**, **high for patch mechanics**.
3. **pi-catalog 18.18.0** — Mistral discovery filter and "a failed refresh no longer replaces a cached catalog with request headers" (`CHANGELOG.md:5-9`). The latter could change which catalog rows exist at runtime after a failed discovery; `image-count-budget` lives on deployment/provider rules, not rows, so budgets are unaffected. Severity **low**; no owner gate needed, arms are the model-cache/registry gates.
4. **No upstream change** to `compat/request-size.ts` (18.16.0 and 18.18.0 bodies are line-identical), to `resolveImageCountBudget`'s signature, or to `snapcompact`'s import of it (`snapcompact.ts:68`, `providerImageBudget:577-586`, floor `DEFAULT_PROVIDER_IMAGE_BUDGET = 5` at `:573`). The cache-prefix invariant this family protects (never rewrite a mid-history image entry) still flows through `clampProviderContextImages`; nothing in the 18.17/18.18 changelogs changes the clamp call sites.
5. **Adjacent, unchanged risk (not a regression):** `deepseek.kdl:16-18` now carries `official-image-count-budget 600` + `catalog-endpoint-budgets #true`; our measured table is host-independent, which is the intended 0052 semantic (it was measured on Command Code routes). Do not widen further.

## 5. Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads > 0`)
**No — nothing here reads main-thread-only state; worker mode can neither crash nor silently re-price.**
- The capability's state is (a) module constants (`MODEL_FRAME_TOKEN_STEPS`, `DEEPSEEK_VISION_FRAME_TOKENS` — patch lines 32-64 of the family patch), (b) pure resolvers (`frameBilling`/`frameTokens`/`frameBillingKey`, `resolveImageCountBudget`), (c) two per-thread memo maps (`imageBudgetCache` `request-size.ts:6-7`, `catalogPolicyCache` `catalog-policy.ts:12-13`). A worker gets its own copy of the same deterministic computation; a cold miss recomputes an equal value — cost is a few map inserts, never a provider request or a cache byte.
- The clamp and snapcompact run inside the thread that owns the session: subagent sessions are real `AgentSession`s built in the worker (`thread/subagent-worker.ts:12-24`; `thread/services/auth.ts:8-22` says the worker builds its own `ModelRegistry` mirror from the same inputs, models.yml/model cache/settings, then follows main's change batches). `imageCountBudget` overrides and runtime-registered providers therefore arrive intact, and `providerType`-based rule resolution is a pure function of the mirrored model (`compat/resolve.ts:1311-1318`).
- The one thread-sensitive seam is `reachesBudgetedHost`, which reads `process.env` (`pi-ai/src/providers/anthropic-state.ts:50-56`, `isFoundryEnabled`): a worker only inherits the env at spawn. If an `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL`/Foundry reroute is set *after* workers exist, main and a worker disagree on whether the official 600-image budget applies — silent over-keep on a gateway (the exact 0044 failure shape: image entries survive but the gateway truncates or 400s) or silent over-eviction. [INFERENCE] on Bun's worker env semantics; no observed crash either way.
- Gate legs to close it (add to `verify-provider-image-budget.ts`, reuse `smokeTestSubagentWorker` at `thread/subagent-worker.ts:618`): (i) inside a spawned worker assert `providerImageBudget` == the main process's value for `pixel/openai-responses` (200), `commandcode-b/commandcode-custom-b` (200), `devin/devin-agent` (200), `umans/openai-responses` (10), `unknown/cursor-agent` (5), and the 601→600 clamp; (ii) a reroute leg that sets `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL` *after* the worker booted and asserts the worker's `providerImageBudget(modern)` is 90, not 600 — this is the "no crash but silently over budget" detector; (iii) negative control in main with the same fixture to prove the leg is live. No patch rewrite is required, only the added assertion.

**Net:** 4/4 capabilities stay; work = verbatim application + one artifact regeneration + version-literal arms + one worker leg. Effort S (≈half a day), risk concentrated entirely in `rules.json`.

# 0094 — `0094-omp-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume` (root AI)

## 1. Family / status on 18.18.0
- Capabilities: **0094** only (manifest `patches/.18.16.0-families/manifest.json:527-544`; owned paths `pi-ai/dist/types/types.d.ts`, `pi-ai/src/providers/anthropic.ts`, `pi-ai/src/types.ts`).
- fuzz-0 on 18.18.0: **PASS, 0 failing hunks**. 12/12 hunks applied at fuzz-0 with offsets only (`replay-logs/0094-omp-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume.txt`: types.d.ts +26/+26, anthropic.ts +7/+19/+27×4/+76×2, types.ts +24/+24). 0094 is absent from main's corrected 12-family failure list (consistent).
- No path-set changes, no new files; patch still touches exactly the 3 manifest paths.

## 2. Failing hunks
None. Every hunk's context still matches byte-for-byte, including the anchor for the new `restorePrefixDroppedThinking(...)` call, which lands right after `getAnthropicProviderSessionState(...)` (`anthropic.ts:2000-2006`) and before `let disableStrictTools` — the same position the patch was written for.

## 3. Capability classification
**0094 → KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL** (effort **S**; **M** only if the thread-mode and worker legs below are added to the gate).

- *Not ADOPT-CANDIDATE*: 18.18.0 has no durable equivalent. Grep of the whole 18.18 tree for `droppedThinkingReplay` yields nothing; the only drop state upstream keeps is the in-memory `prefixDroppedThinkingBlocks: Set<string>` (`anthropic.ts:387`, created `:411`, `.clear()` on close `:424`, re-hydrated only `??= new Set()` on existing states `:519`). Negative control proving non-adoption: kill the process after a 400 repair, resume from session JSONL, observe the blocks replay (nothing persists the set). Retiring 0094 would be a regression.
- Rebase method: apply the 18.16.0 patch unchanged (offsets are cosmetic). Two edits are needed, both in the gate: add `"18.18.0"` to the supported-version allowlist and to `serverManagedDrops` (`patches/verify-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume.ts:20-21`). Without it the gate aborts `unsupported thinking-drop resume gate version: 18.18.0` (pi-ai `package.json` version is exactly `18.18.0`).
- Gate: `_omp_gate 0094 verify-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume.ts` (`patches/apply.sh:554`); 0094 is in `INDEPENDENT_IDS` (`patches/verify-family-plan.py:59`), so no family-plan edit.
- Semantic re-check done, all consumers intact at 18.18.0:
  - serializer still consumes the set by stable key — `droppedThinkingBlocks` param `anthropic.ts:5060`, wire-in `:5172`, `:5416`, thinking skip `:6026-6030`, redacted skip `:6068-6069` (identical to 18.16.0:5898-5941);
  - the set is still passed on every params build `anthropic.ts:2282`;
  - prefix-binding 400 detection/recovery unchanged; `dropAllThinking` fallback when no state `anthropic.ts:3406-3414`; the latest-assistant-turn repair still adds keys **without** a durable record `anthropic.ts:3448-3466` (post-restart that path may 400 again — pre-existing 18.16.0 behavior, not a regression; worth noting in the plan);
  - `output` is one object mutated in place for the whole call, so the record cannot be lost by reassignment; no 18.18 retry site deletes it (reset sites clear only `inputTransformations`/`providerPayload`: `:3419-3423`, `:3462-3466`, `:3538-3542`, `:3723-3727`);
  - scope inputs unchanged: `injectedClientBaseUrl` `anthropic-client.ts:80-83` (undefined ⇒ neither persist nor restore, matching gate leg 11), `AnthropicMessagesClient#baseURL` getter `anthropic-client.ts:204-207`, session-state keying `anthropic.ts:507-519`;
  - metadata never reaches the wire: the field lives only on `AssistantMessage` (`src/types.ts`, patch `@@ -1377,6 +1396,12 @@`), which `convertAnthropicMessages` never serializes wholesale;
  - persistence round-trips unknown fields: `sessionManager.appendMessage` → `prepareEntryForPersistence` only rewrites `content`/`details` (`session-persistence.ts:534-583`); no zod/whitelist on read (`session-entries.ts`); `normalizeSessionMessageForProviderReplay` does not list the field (`session/messages.ts:324-360`), so adding it cannot trip `didSessionMessagesChange` and cause prefix churn.

## 4. Upstream 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes near the owned paths
Only one genuinely new neighbor; everything else the patch sits on already existed in 18.16.0.

1. **NEW: Anthropic message threads / stateful responses — severity MEDIUM (cost, not correctness).**
   `pi-ai/src/providers/anthropic-threads.ts` does not exist in the 18.16.0 tree; 18.18.0 imports it (`anthropic.ts:201-206`), adds `threads: AnthropicThreadChains` to provider session state (`:414-420`, `:424-426`), and picks `threadTurn` when a session id is set (`:2255-2267`). Gated by `supportsMessageThreads` + official endpoint (`anthropic-threads.ts:140-150`); the catalog enables it **by default for first-party Anthropic ≥4.5** (`pi-catalog/src/compat/rules/classes/anthropic.kdl:220-221`, axis `compat/axes.ts:278`, default `false` in `compat/resolve.ts:906-907`).
   - Interaction: `extendsConversation` compares the held prefix deeply (`anthropic-threads.ts:354-358`), so a client-side drop inside an already-held message turns the next request from `continue` into `create` (full re-upload + one prefix re-cache). One-time, self-correcting; the anchor stores the *already dropped* body, so a resumed process (no chain) creates a full body with the same drops and the same prefix bytes.
   - Ordering is safe: a prefix-binding 400 carries no thread error code, so `threadTurn.recover` (`anthropic.ts:3282`) falls through and `dropAfterUnexplained400` (`:3680`) runs only after our branch (`:3406-3414`).
   - Owning gate: 0094 — but the gate never passes `sessionId` (`verify-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume.ts:172-185`), so **thread mode is uncovered**. Add one leg with `sessionId` asserting (a) drops still applied on a `create`, (b) a mid-session drop yields `create`, never a silently divergent `continue`.
2. **No change** to the invariants themselves: block-binding request field and `prefixMismatchBehavior` resolution identical (`anthropic.ts:2032-2036`, `:5321-5331` vs 18.16.0:2006-2013, `:5212-5214`); `context_management clear_thinking_20251015` and legacy compaction-replay edit already in 18.16.0 (`anthropic.ts:5363-5374` vs 18.16.0:5246-5258) — owned by `family-compaction`; fallback handoff, Claude-Code-version retry, `applyCompactionIterationUsage`, opaque-client anchor + fast-mode rearm all already present in 18.16.0 (`:392-512`, `:1470`, `:1885`, `:3304`). No OAuth surface in this family.
3. Gate fixtures still valid: `claude-opus-5-5` remains the anthropic default model (`pi-catalog/src/compat/rules/classes/anthropic.kdl:4`), and the gate's `fallbacks: [{model}]` body still sanitizes the same way (`anthropic.ts:2064-2075`).

## 5. Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads`, default 4)
**No** — the capability holds no process-global or main-thread-only state.
- Evidence: the drop set lives in the *caller-supplied* `providerSessionState` map (`anthropic.ts:2000-2005`, `:2282`), which each `AgentSession` creates for itself (`session/agent-session.ts:1442`, getter `:3422-3424`); the durable record is plain JSON on the assistant message, restored from `context.messages` per request (patch-inserted `restorePrefixDroppedThinking`, `anthropic.ts:509-545` of the patch). A subagent worker is a separate realm with its own module graph (`thread/subagent-worker.ts:36-38`) that builds and owns its own `AgentSession`/`SessionManager` and publishes message copies as plain data (`thread/session-publisher.ts:1-48`, `:369-387`) — no symbols, Sets, timers.
- Failure mode if it *had* relied on main-thread state: silent — a fresh realm replays rejected thinking blocks, takes a 400, spends one extra retry (tokens + a new prefix write) on every resume; never a crash.
- Gate legs carrying this: scenarios 2–4 (JSON round-trip + fresh `new Map()`) are the in-process analogue of a new realm (`verify-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume.ts:234-270`). To make it literal, add one leg running the patched tree inside `new Worker(...)`; expected PASS with no code change.

## Action items for the plan
1. Re-apply the patch unchanged (mechanical, S).
2. Gate version lists: `["…","18.16.0","18.18.0"]` and `serverManagedDrops` (`verify-anthropic-thinking-drop-resume.ts:20-21`).
3. New gate coverage: thread mode (`sessionId` set) and one worker-realm leg.
4. Update `PATCHES.md:1007-1012` wording to the new baseline once the patch SHA is regenerated.
5. No retirement, no path retirement, nothing removed from `apply.sh`.

No OAuth or cache-prefix *bytes* changed on this family's paths: `compaction.ts`, `anthropic.ts`, `openai-codex-state.ts` field set are unchanged (`disableWebsocket` still exists, 18.18.0 `:185`).

## 5. Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads`, default 4 — `task/settings.ts:438-442`)
Verdict: **no capability shares mutable compaction state across threads, but three mechanisms go silently wrong with `workerThreads > 0`.**
- Realm model (evidence): a worker-homed session runs its own `AgentSession`; main only proxies it (`thread/remote-agent-session.ts:5-23,247`, `session-publisher.ts:493-494`, `abortCompaction` only, `:275,603`). Therefore `compaction.ts`'s `agent.state.messages` identity checks, `#buildCachePreservingContext`'s `sessionManager.getLeafId()`/`promptGeneration()` comparisons, and pi-ai's `getCodexWebSocketSessionState` map + `codexWebSocketGeneration` all live in one isolate with the socket they describe. `instanceof CodexCompactionChainLostError` never crosses the RPC (compaction itself is never an RPC method). So: **no crash, no cross-thread baseline confusion.**
- (a) `compaction-trace.ts:12-24` — process-wide file, record carries `pid` only. N workers append with the same pid, no thread/agent identity → records indistinguishable; concurrent `appendFileSync` (O_APPEND) won't crash but line ownership is lost. **Silent diagnostic loss** — precisely the class where "cache didn't attach" becomes unprovable. Gate leg: parse the trace as JSONL after 2 concurrent worker compactions and require a distinct `threadId`+`agentId` per record.
- (b) `task/executor.ts:1245-1265` pins `compaction.midTurnEnabled: true` and (ours) `compaction.experimentalContextManagement: false` in a child overlay. Settings reach a worker as a *mirror of main* plus "overlays created from a mirror in the worker are ordinary local overlays" (`thread/services/settings.ts:6-16`, `thread/protocol.ts:36-38`). Whether the executor's `derived` overlay rides into `SubagentWorkerInit.settings` is **[INFERENCE]** — not proven from source here. If it is dropped, a worker subagent silently loses mid-turn compaction (long runs sail past the threshold → provider overflow, `executor.ts:1257-1262`) and may gain the notes-backed context window the pin was meant to exclude. Gate leg: spawn a worker subagent, assert both settings inside the worker.
- (c) Our `lastClose`/`lastPeerClose` bookkeeping is per-process today and would become per-thread; that is correct *if* rewritten onto `controlFrames.closed` per §3/0070 — if instead it is left on a shared module map, a peer close on one agent's socket could mark another agent's baseline dead (silent chain loss on the healthy one).
- Gate to add: one worker-mode leg per owning gate — run the real compaction inside a worker (`task.workerThreads>0`), assert (i) `httpSends === 0` (no SSE replay), (ii) `canAppend` transitions exactly as the same-thread leg, (iii) a trace record exists with unique thread identity, (iv) no `Cannot send … instance across threads` rejection. That is the "not crash but silently costs money / doesn't attach cache" detector the task asks for.

## 6. Net plan deltas for this family
1. Replay budget: 12 real failures, but re-author **all 33 hunks** of `openai-codex-responses.ts` (offsets up to −585 prove mis-anchoring).
2. New owned path required: `pi-ai/src/providers/responses-websocket-connection.ts` (peer-close hook) — must be added to the 18.18.0 manifest or 0070's diagnosis stays blind.
3. Re-pin 8 gate allowlists to `18.18.0`; add the worker-mode legs of §5 to `verify-codex-compaction-chain*.ts`, `verify-compaction-cache-liveness.ts`, `verify-compaction-lane-trace.ts`.
4. Effort: 0035 S, 0063 M, 0067 M, 0068 M–L, 0070 L, 0088 M, 0089 S, 0096 M → ~4–5 focused days with gates.

`src/session/agent-session.ts @@ -1282,6` inserts `#inputPreparationAbortController` + `#admittedExtensionAppends` after `#idleEphemeralReservation?: { abort; settled }`. That field is **not upstream 18.18.0** (grep `idleEphemeral` over the whole 18.18.0 package tree: 0 hits; 18.16.0 pristine `agent-session.ts` also has 0 hits) — it is supplied by family `0010-omp-extension-idle-ephemeral-turn` in the layered tree, and 0010 itself takes 5 FAILED hunks. Rebase anchor: insert after `#inFlightEpoch = 0;` (`agent-session.ts:1368`). Not a logic rewrite.

No hunk in this family fails because upstream "already does it", and no hunk needs a semantic redesign.

### 2. Capability classification

**0082 — auto-translate ordinary input, UI keeps the original (`displayText` + awaited `prepare_user_input`)**
- Class: **KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL** (S). All 7 failures are (A)/(B)/(C); the logic hunks all apply, including the two wire-strip points (`pi-agent-core/src/agent.ts` `defaultConvertToLlm` strip; `pi-agent-core/src/compaction/messages.ts` `case "user"` delete) and the input-controller submission hook.
- Rebase method: regenerate the runner `.d.ts` long import line from the 18.18.0 line plus `PrepareUserInputEvent, PrepareUserInputResult`; re-insert the four `AgentViewSession`-adjacent import lines by hand; un-delete the `isDateCwdReminderControl` import against the `CostGateApi` context line; re-anchor the two new private fields on `#inFlightEpoch = 0;`. Then re-run fuzz-0.
- Gates: `verify-user-display-text-queue.ts` — registered for every `OMP_PATCH_VERSION != 18.16.0` (`patches/apply.sh:556-561`) but its version allowlist stops at 18.12.6 (`verify-user-display-text-queue.ts:42-47`) → **fails closed on 18.18.0**; must add `18.18.0` (and `hasQueueRemoval`/`hasQueuePolicySplit` arms). The end-to-end translator/`prepare_user_input` gate `verify-extensions-load-18160.ts` is hard-pinned to `agentVersion === "18.16.0"` with `OMP_PATCH_VERSION ∈ {undefined, 18.16.0}` (`verify-extensions-load-18160.ts:66-74`, registered `apply.sh:601-604`) → 18.18.0 would ship this capability with **no** v1-contract gate unless re-armed. Effort for gate re-arm: M (legs 7-8 + translator legs + pristine negative controls are version-sensitive: `verify-extensions-load-18160.ts:539-543` already special-cases the 18.16.0 date/cwd journal).

**0085 — rejected prepared submission / cancelled preparation restore**
- Class: **KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL** (S). Zero 0085-owned hunks fail (`draft.ts`, `main.ts`, `input-controller.ts` restore paths all apply). The two load-bearing duck types survive: `AgentStartPolicyChangedError` still exists with `this.name` set (`agent-session.ts:629-633`) and is still thrown at `:9114`; `PromptDroppedError` unchanged (`:645-650`). 
- Rebase method: none beyond the family-wide reapplication; re-verify that the `main.ts` submit path still throws before `admitSubmission` in the same shape (`agent-session.ts:8410` `prompt` → `#admitSubmission`).
- Gate: `verify-rejected-prepared-draft-restore.ts`, whose allowlist ends at `"18.16.0"` (`:88`) → must add `18.18.0`; its legs 11-12 (late preparation after reset, real loop replay) need re-baselining because 18.18.0's `#beginSessionTransition` gained/kept transition call sites (`agent-session.ts:6277`, used at `:6876, 11774, 11928, 13510, 13948, 14086, 14248`). Effort: S.

**Retired items:** none in this family (0065/0025/0100 live elsewhere).

### 3. Worker-thread assumption (item 5)

**Answer: No** — this family does not rely on process-global or main-thread-only state, with one path-scoped caveat.
- All new state is per-`AgentSession` instance (`#inputPreparationAbortController`, `#admittedExtensionAppends`, `#queuedMessageRawText` WeakMap) or per-`InputController` (`#preparationTail`), and `appendExtensionEntryPersisted`/`inputPreparationSignal` are instance members.
- `prepare_user_input` is emitted only by the interactive host: the only call site is `InputController` (`input-controller.ts`, patch hunk at 1091+, `runner.emitPrepareUserInput`), and focused-subagent input returns before it (`input-controller.ts:1105-1109`, `:1626` `#submitToFocusedSession`, `:2099-2102` follow-up path). Subagents in workers therefore never translate — correct, and no extra request.
- `displayText` is a plain data field: it would cross the worker RPC intact (rpc encodes property bags and `structuredClone`s them, `src/thread/rpc.ts:906-910`), and prompt options cross as plain objects (`src/thread/session-mirror.ts:324`).
- **Caveat (unclear → watch):** under worker mode a focused *worker-hosted* subagent view resolves `extensionRunner` to the main ancestor's runner (`src/thread/remote-agent-session.ts:436-439`), whereas 18.16.0 had no such proxy (no `PeerAgentSession` class exists in 18.16.0). If a future 18.18.x path routed focused input through the normal submit path, it would emit `prepare_user_input` into main's translator with `target: "main"` (hardcoded in the patch) — failure mode is **silent cost** (one extra DeepSeek translation request per focused submit), not a crash and not cache loss. Current guard makes it unreachable; the plan's worker gate should nonetheless assert that a focused-subagent submit issues zero `prepare_user_input` emissions.

### 4. Upstream 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes near owned paths that can silently break invariants

1. **Compaction prefix slice (High).** The family replaces the control-skipping loop in the side-request context provider with a plain `messages.slice(0, prefixLength)` (patch hunk `@@ -2826`, "Persisted date/cwd anchors stamp existing messages; they add no controls"). Upstream 18.18.0 **still creates** date/cwd control messages (`session/date-cwd-reminder.ts:142-146`, `:444-446`) and still counts past them (`agent-session.ts:2943-2950`). The claim only holds if family `persistent-prefix-resume` (0047; **10 FAILED hunks**) keeps stamping anchors instead of controls. If that rebase lands wrong, the summarizer prefix is short by N developer controls → live-chain prefix no longer byte-identical to the main chain → silent cache miss on every compaction. Responsible gates: 0047's `verify-date-cwd-journal-prefix.ts` (18.16.0 arm; the non-18.16.0 arm `verify-date-cwd-anchor-stability.ts`, `apply.sh:504-509`) plus `verify-compaction-cache-liveness.ts` / `verify-prefix-cache-compaction.ts`.
2. **New block renderer path (`transcript-presentation`) — Medium, visual only.** Upstream added `messageBlock`/`PresentedBlock` (`ui-helpers.ts:46`) and hand-built user rows elsewhere; if the new presentation path renders user rows without `userDisplayText`, the transcript silently shows the English canonical text. Gates: the display-text gate's resume/queue legs do not cover rendering; add a leg (or accept as cosmetic).
3. **Wire purity is only asserted on `content` (Medium).** Both gates check `JSON.stringify(wire[0].content)` / `textContent(user.content)` (`verify-extensions-load-18160.ts:543`, `verify-rejected-prepared-draft-restore.ts:602`) — a `displayText` riding as a sibling property of a provider `Message` would change request bytes without failing either gate. Provider clones operate on already-stripped `Message` objects (`pi-ai/src/providers/anthropic-images.ts:212`, `anthropic-threads.ts:454`), so no known live leak, but the 18.18.0 gate should assert `JSON.stringify(wireMessage)` excludes the original text.
4. **Transition funnel (`Low`).** The abort hook lives inside `#beginSessionTransition` (`agent-session.ts:6277`), which every transition in 18.18.0 still uses (7 call sites listed above) — no silent bypass today; any future transition not routed through it would let a late translation land in the wrong transcript (the 0085 leg-11 regression).
5. **OAuth / idle warm-up / Codex `textVerbosity`:** this family touches none of `pi-ai/src/auth/*`, no cache-warmer, no codex provider files (owned-path list in `manifest.json:363-420` confirms). No interaction.

**Bottom line:** 0082 → KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL, 0085 → KEEP-REBASE-MECHANICAL, 7 mechanical hunks, S effort for the patch, M for re-arming the two version-pinned gates (plus adding `18.18.0` to both allowlists); the only semantic coupling to watch is the date/cwd anchor invariant shared with `persistent-prefix-resume` and `family-compaction`.

Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads`, default 4)\n**No module/process-global state of our own.** Every piece this family adds is per-AgentSession:\n`#transcriptReadOnly`, `#recallGeneration`, `#mentalModelsLoadGeneration` on `HindsightSessionState`\n(state.ts:243-285), `#baseSystemPromptPromotionTail` on `SessionMemory` (session-memory.ts:172-206),\n`#memoryTranscriptReadOnly` on `AgentSession` (agent-session.ts:1462 area). No timers, no extension\nrunner, no auth store, no session registry.\n\n**Alias path is closed in a worker**: a taskDepth>0 subagent builds its own concrete\n`HindsightSessionState` with `aliasOf = Remote…` (backend.ts:43-56) and `hasRecalledForFirstTurn:\ntrue`, so `beforeAgentStartPrompt` returns `undefined`; `#resetHindsightConversationTrackingIfHindsight`\nreturns false on `state.aliasOf` (session-memory.ts:234), so no reload/persist runs cross-thread. Any\nmarker it did write goes to the worker's own `SessionManager` (real `open`/`forkFrom`/`inMemory` —\nexecutor.ts:4813-4827), never to main's branch.\n\n**One inherited cross-thread hazard (upstream, not ours)** — answer \"unclear for the invariant\": the\nalias's `mentalModelsSnippet` is main's live value (memory-remote.ts:198-200). With\n`workerThreads>0`, a `/memory mm reload` (or a main transcript boundary) on main changes every live\nsubagent's developer instructions at block 3 → the subagent's provider prefix misses from that block\non (silent cost, **not** a crash), and the `callSync` blocks the worker while main is busy.\nFailure mode: silent cache loss / worker stall.\n**Gate leg to add** (both gates are single-process today): open one `task` with `workerThreads=4`,\nsnapshot the subagent's `session_init.systemPrompt`, reload MM on main, and assert (a) the subagent's\nnext request system blocks are byte-identical, (b) zero `hindsight_recall_snippet` /\n`hindsight_mental_models_snippet` entries appear on the parent branch, (c) the parent's own promotion\ntail is still exactly one part. A leg that only asserts \"no exception\" would be red-free and useless.\n\n## Environment safety\nRead-only: only `read`/`grep`/`glob` over pristine trees and the patch repo; no omp run, no `~/.omp`,\nno DB/credentials, no patch application (the fuzz-0 counts come from the pre-existing\nreplay-logs/family-hindsight.txt, cross-checked against the corrected baseline for 6 hunks)."
}

Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads` > 0)\n**No — the capability holds no process-global or main-thread-only state.**\n- Reservation, epoch and counters are instance fields (`agent-session.ts:1363-1368`); every gate predicate is a session method: `isStreaming`, `hasAdmittedSubmission` (`:3613`), `isSessionTransitioning` (`:6268`), `queuedMessageCount`, `#idleMaintenance.isRecapRunning` (patch adds the getter). The worker's `AsyncLocalStorage` blocker (`runner.ts:549`) is a separate isolate instance.\n- Idle maintenance on a worker-hosted session is driven from main over RPC (`thread/remote-agent-session.ts:804-816`) but its predicate is evaluated inside the worker against the same local `AgentSession`, so `hasIdleEphemeralReservation` is never read cross-thread; `RemoteAgentSession`/`AgentSessionHandle` expose only `runEphemeralTurn` (`remote-agent-session.ts:792`, `agent-session-handle.ts:393`) — no new RPC method needed.\n- Failure mode with `workerThreads>0`: none for the invariant (no crash, no silent cache break). The only cross-thread behaviour is legitimate: main and subagent sessions can each hold their own reservation in different threads and issue two billed side requests under different `promptCacheKey`s.\n- Gate leg that would catch a regression: `verify-idle-ephemeral-reservation.ts:129-135` (side-settled-before-main-dispatch) and `:266-269` (tree-entry refusal) are main-thread only. Add a worker leg: spawn a subagent with `task.workerThreads: 1`, have the child extension call `ctx.runIdleEphemeralTurn` while the child streams, assert `undefined` plus zero provider calls — that catches \"no crash, but a duplicate request was billed\".\n\n## Files\n- `patches/.18.16.0-families/0010-omp-extension-idle-ephemeral-turn-v18.16.0.patch` — failing hunks at patch lines 45-60 (A), 253-260 (B), 353-367 (C), 603-637 (D), 990-1008 (E).\n- 18.18.0 evidence: `src/session/agent-session.ts:1363-1368,1477-1516,3608-3615,6279-6285,7046-7101,9150,11675,13169-13200,14334`; `src/session/idle-maintenance.ts:44-58,86-114,162-189,316-318`; `dist/types/session/agent-session.d.ts:331-333`; `src/thread/remote-agent-session.ts:792,804-816`; `src/task/executor.ts:557-563,4377-4385,5217`; `src/task/settings.ts:438-442`; `CHANGELOG.md:5,66,79,87,130,135`.\n- Gates: `patches/verify-idle-ephemeral-reservation.ts:34,37,56,129-135,266-269,406-456,586-646`; `patches/verify-session-cache-keepalive.ts:151,380-387`; `patches/apply.sh:526,586`."

Each thread therefore gets an independent `effortControls` map by construction — including on the main thread.\n- The only cross-thread carrier is the durable snapshot inside `providerPayload`, which survives the session file and the RPC mirror (`src/rpc/wire/content.ts:121` declares `providerPayload?: Record<string, unknown>`; nothing in `src/thread/**` strips it — grep returns no matches).\n- A worker-thread subagent whose `parentSessionId` names a main-thread session simply finds no local parent state (`hasParentState === false`) and restores from its own history, which is the intended path; it does not need the parent's map.\nFailure mode *if* this assumption ever broke: no crash — silent baseline drift, i.e. a per-turn `reasoning.effort` change against a server that already holds a `configuration_update` chain ⇒ prompt-cache miss on every turn (repeated full-price input) and possibly a `configuration_update`-adjacent rejection. No writes, no `omp`, no `~/.omp`, `~/.bun`, `agent.db`/`models.db`, credential or cache access, no network."

Compaction wire shape unchanged: `compaction: {type:\"summarize\"}` (`anthropic.ts:5488-5493`), legacy edit `compact_20260112` (`:1469`, `:1476`), replay block `type:\"compaction\"` (`:1410-1418`) — the audit's `native-compaction`/`compaction-replay` kinds stay valid; severity none.\n5. `anthropic-images.ts` unchanged upstream and the 18.18 \"thin tool images are padded\" fix is not in pi-ai (no match for padding/minimum-height in `pi-ai/src`) ⇒ image-prefix invariant unaffected; severity none.\n6. Cache-facing **session** changes: unknown `config.yml` keys no longer reset settings and drop tools (`pi-coding-agent/CHANGELOG.md:66`), cache-miss marker now detects tail re-sends (`:65`), `cache` status segment added (`:22`). Informational only; no write-path change. One-time cold cache after upgrade due to system-prompt/skill-hint changes (`CHANGELOG.md:76,178`) — expected, no gate.\n7. `textVerbosity` default low (`CHANGELOG.md:121`) affects Codex only, not this family.\n\n### 5. Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads`, default 4)\n**Answer: no hard main-thread-only dependency, but two concrete cross-thread hazards.**\nEvidence for \"no\": the whole capability is per-request state — `createAnthropicRequestAudit` state is a closure local (`anthropic-request-audit.ts`, `let current`), `applyPromptCaching` is pure over `params`, and the refs end up in `preparedContext`/`params` only (`anthropic.ts:2268-2330`). Subagents placed in worker threads run a full `AgentSession` inside that worker (`task/executor.ts:4383-4386`, `thread/subagent-worker.ts:19-60`), which builds its own `#providerSessionState` (`session/agent-session.ts:1442,2800`) — state is per-isolate, nothing is shared.\nHazards:\n- **H1 (silent, diagnostics only):** audit identity is `sha256(sessionId ?? promptCacheKey, provider, endpoint, model)`; same-type sibling subagents may share one `promptCacheKey` (`agent-session.ts:13271`, `getPromptCacheKeyForModel`). Two workers with no `sessionId` would `renameSync` onto the same `<identity>.baseline.json` and then compare full-vs-appended thread bodies, producing false `prefix-different` rows. Failure mode: **no crash, no cost** — corrupted diagnostics, so the audit can no longer prove the cache chain. Not caught by any leg: `verify-anthropic-request-audit.ts:98-109` is single-threaded and always passes `sessionId: \"audit-session\"`.\n- **H2 (CPU only):** `anthropicImageResizeCaches` is a module-level `Map` (`anthropic-images.ts:81`), so each worker re-encodes the same screenshots. Output is deterministic (`Bun.Image … png()/jpeg()` with fixed quality, `anthropic-images.ts:141-143`, no timestamps/randomness), so the prefix still matches — **silent extra CPU, not extra tokens**. Uncatchable today because both gates run in one realm.\n- Also relevant to H2/H1: the hunk-1 import break crashes on every thread equally, so a worker-thread gate would have caught it; the current gates cannot.\nGate legs that close this (implementation-phase work):\n1. Worker leg in `verify-anthropic-cache-boundaries.ts`: run the same fixture on the main thread and inside ≥2 `Bun.Worker`s with `task.workerThreads` semantics, with per-call markers **off** and **on**, and assert byte-identical captures (message breakpoint indexes, image dims, system head) — catches \"cache not connected\" that a crash-only check misses.\n2. Concurrency leg in `verify-anthropic-request-audit.ts`: two concurrent `streamAnthropic` calls with the same `promptCacheKey` and no `sessionId`, asserting two distinct baselines (or a `threadId/pid`-qualified identity) and that neither call reports `prefix-different`.\n3. Make both gates PASS with `task.workerThreads=4`; if either leg cannot be made to pass, ship with `0` plus the reason (per DECISIONS).\n\nNotes: upstream commit subjects were not obtainable from this worker (no shell tool; `upstream.git` is a bare pack). Provenance is asserted from the shipped 18.18.0 `CHANGELOG.md`/`pi-ai/CHANGELOG.md` entries and code anchors above; PR/commit mapping is `[INFERENCE]` from those entries."
}

our rebase must not re-introduce a settings-triggered tool change | prefix-cache |\n| LOW | `buildCompactionV2Headers` drops `BETA_RESPONSES` (`compaction-v2-streaming.ts:421-485`) | none (header only) | chain §1 wire assert |\n| LOW | `preferWebsockets(model)` host signature (`session-maintenance.ts:412`, `session-advisors.ts:490`) | compile/context only | tsc type probe |\n| LOW | `./c` continue after compaction (`CHANGELOG.md:63,68`) | none | — |\n\nNo OAuth or cache-prefix *bytes* changed on this family's paths: `compaction.ts`, `anthropic.ts`, `openai-codex-state.ts` field set are unchanged (`disableWebsocket` still exists, 18.18.0 `:185`).\n\n## 5. Worker-thread assumption (`task.workerThreads`, default 4 — `task/settings.ts:438-442`)\nVerdict: **no capability shares mutable compaction state across threads, but three mechanisms go silently wrong with `workerThreads > 0`.**\n- Realm model (evidence): a worker-homed session runs its own `AgentSession`; main only proxies it (`thread/remote-agent-session.ts:5-23,247`, `session-publisher.ts:493-494`, `abortCompaction` only, `:275,603`). Therefore `compaction.ts`'s `agent.state.messages` identity checks, `#buildCachePreservingContext`'s `sessionManager.getLeafId()`/`promptGeneration()` comparisons, and pi-ai's `getCodexWebSocketSessionState` map + `codexWebSocketGeneration` all live in one isolate with the socket they describe. `instanceof CodexCompactionChainLostError` never crosses the RPC (compaction itself is never an RPC method). So: **no crash, no cross-thread baseline confusion.**\n- (a) `compaction-trace.ts:12-24` — process-wide file, record carries `pid` only. N workers append with the same pid, no thread/agent identity → records indistinguishable; concurrent `appendFileSync` (O_APPEND) won't crash but line ownership is lost. **Silent diagnostic loss** — precisely the class where \"cache didn't attach\" becomes unprovable. Gate leg: parse the trace as JSONL after 2 concurrent worker compactions and require a distinct `threadId`+`agentId` per record.\n- (b) `task/executor.ts:1245-1265` pins `compaction.midTurnEnabled: true` and (ours) `compaction.experimentalContextManagement: false` in a child overlay. Settings reach a worker as a *mirror of main* plus \"overlays created from a mirror in the worker are ordinary local overlays\" (`thread/services/settings.ts:6-16`, `thread/protocol.ts:36-38`). Whether the executor's `derived` overlay rides into `SubagentWorkerInit.settings` is **[INFERENCE]** — not proven from source here. If it is dropped, a worker subagent silently loses mid-turn compaction (long runs sail past the threshold → provider overflow, `executor.ts:1257-1262`) and may gain the notes-backed context window the pin was meant to exclude. Gate leg: spawn a worker subagent, assert both settings inside the worker.\n- (c) Our `lastClose`/`lastPeerClose` bookkeeping is per-process today and would become per-thread; that is correct *if* rewritten onto `controlFrames.closed` per §3/0070 — if instead it is left on a shared module map, a peer close on one agent's socket could mark another agent's baseline dead (silent chain loss on the healthy one).\n- Gate to add: one worker-mode leg per owning gate — run the real compaction inside a worker (`task.workerThreads>0`), assert (i) `httpSends === 0` (no SSE replay), (ii) `canAppend` transitions exactly as the same-thread leg, (iii) a trace record exists with unique thread identity, (iv) no `Cannot send … instance across threads` rejection. That is the \"not crash but silently costs money / doesn't attach cache\" detector the task asks for.\n\n## 6. Net plan deltas for this family\n1. Replay budget: 12 real failures, but re-author **all 33 hunks** of `openai-codex-responses.ts` (offsets up to −585 prove mis-anchoring).\n2. New owned path required: `pi-ai/src/providers/responses-websocket-connection.ts` (peer-close hook) — must be added to the 18.18.0 manifest or 0070's diagnosis stays blind.\n3. Re-pin 8 gate allowlists to `18.18.0`; add the worker-mode legs of §5 to `verify-codex-compaction-chain*.ts`, `verify-compaction-cache-liveness.ts`, `verify-compaction-lane-trace.ts`.\n4. Effort: 0035 S, 0063 M, 0067 M, 0068 M–L, 0070 L, 0088 M, 0089 S, 0096 M → ~4–5 focused days with gates.\n"

No CONFLICT: nothing upstream forbids the env budget or parking.\n\n**Gates need work, not just passing:** `verify-cache-warming-idle-budget.ts` calls `w.start({...}, () => true)` — 18.18 interprets the second arg as `change()` where a truthy value means \"context changed\", so the gate must pass `() => undefined` and fire the returned `dispatched()` closure; `apply.sh:584-587` registers the cron/keepalive gates **only when `OMP_PATCH_VERSION == 18.16.0`**, so on 18.18.0 they silently disappear unless re-pointed (acceptance criterion 2: retired gates gone, live gates present).\n\n## 4. Upstream 18.16.0→18.18.0 changes near the owned paths that can break the invariant even when hunks apply\n1. **Entry lifetime anchored at dispatch**, not at `start`: `start` returns `dispatched()` (`cache-warmer.ts:475-529`), session stores it per context (`agent-session.ts:6048-6055`), stream `onPayload` fires it (`sdk.ts:4900-4903`). Severity **high** (our park/resume anchoring silently wrong → full-prefix re-write).\n2. **`isCurrent` → `change()`** (`cache-warmer.ts:275`, `:470-473`, `:479`, `:683`). Severity **high** (timer `TypeError` = crash, or silently \"context changed\" stop).\n3. **`refreshSentAt` / `queuedAt` / `sentAt`** (`:289`, `:672`, `:700-701`, `:764-766`): \"refreshing\" now means dispatched. Our `settling` counter spans from before dispatch — deliberate, but see 6.\n4. **Miss path keeps the entry**: `#touch` on `cacheWrite > 0` then stop (`:709-717`). Our `report(\"miss\")` + stop must not also clear the parked anchor. Severity medium.\n5. **`stopsAt` in the public status** (`:433` → `prompt-cache-status.ts:61`) bypasses the env horizon. Severity medium (observability/UI only, no cost).\n6. **Idle recap re-gated on settlement and armed after it** (`idle-maintenance.ts:219-227`, `:318`, `:349`). If a warm replay is folded into `#isSettled`/`waitForSessionSettlement`, `:225` returns false and **nothing is armed** — the recap and idle compaction starve for the whole warmed/parked window (up to the entry TTL). Severity **high** (silent feature loss, no crash). Keep the replay in `#isQuiet` + the per-step `BUSY_RETRY_MS` retry instead.\n7. **`runIdleCompaction` now cancels the warmer** when not streaming/compacting (`agent-session.ts:7647`) — `cancel()` must drop `#parked` (ours does via `#stop`→`#clearRun`→`#dropParked`; assert it in the gate).\n8. **Compaction lead**: idle compaction still fires 5 s before the warmer's refresh point (`idle-maintenance.ts:263-277`). A parked run that resumes late makes the summary read a cold prefix. Severity medium; covered by `verify-cron-idle-owners.ts`.\n9. `CacheWarmingResultEvent` must join the `ExtensionEvent` union and `ExtensionContext.on` overload (`src/extensibility/extensions/types.ts:1696`, `:1903` in 18.16 numbering; re-locate in 18.18). Mechanical.\n\n## 5. Worker-thread assumption (task.workerThreads, default 4)\n**No — 0092 does not assume main-thread-only state that worker threads would break, and the warmer never runs off the main thread**, by construction:\n- Every spawned/subagent session opts out: `task/executor.ts:4944-4945` (\"Subagents are short-lived; never schedule background warm requests. `cacheWarming: false`\"), also `thread/worker-warm-up.ts:62`, `compress/session.ts:47`, `commit/agentic/agent.ts:65`. With `cacheWarming:false` no `CacheWarmer` is constructed (`sdk.ts:4808-4811`) → no parking, no `#settling`, no `cache_warming_result` emission in a worker.\n- Everything 0092 adds is per-instance state: `#settling`, `#parked` (`cache-warmer.ts`), `#recapSettling` (`idle-maintenance.ts`), and the `AgentSession` getters `idleRecapRunning`/`cacheWarmReplaying`. No module-level map, no session registry, no auth store, no extension runner beyond the existing `extensionRunner.emit` the session already owns (`sdk.ts:4817-4818`).\n- The one genuine process-global is `process.env.PI_CACHE_WARMING_IDLE_MAX_MS` (`cache-warmer.ts` const + `idleWarmingAgeMs()`, 18.18 target `:62-80`). Worker threads get their own `process.env` copy unless `SHARE_ENV` (Node semantics; Bun parity not verified here) [INFERENCE], so if a warmer were ever constructed in a worker it would read the 30-minute default and **silently not keep alive** (cost, not crash) while the cron extension's `/cron keep` reports success. That is the failure mode to guard, and today it is unreachable because of `cacheWarming:false`.\n- **Gate legs to add** (none exist today — `verify-cache-warming-idle-budget.ts` is single-threaded and would not catch a cross-thread divergence): (i) assert a task-executor-spawned session has `cacheWarmer === undefined` while `task.workerThreads` is 4, (ii) drive the warmer in-process while a `Bun.Worker` subagent is alive and assert the parked run still hits its 54-minute refresh (no cancel, no missed arm), (iii) assert `idleWarmingAgeMs()` in a spawned worker and in the main thread agree, so the env dependency is proven inert or replaced by an injected `deps.getIdleHorizonMs` (preferred long-term fix). `verify-cron-idle-owners.ts` already pins \"the host warmer defers to an in-flight idle recap\" and is the natural home for (ii).\n"

`sdk.ts:3747` builds one `ExtensionRunner` per `createAgentSession` | Low — no shared mutable state in *our* patch; consumer duplicates its own maps per thread | none |\n| Main-turn path unchanged: `Agent` calls `transformContext(messages, signal)` only | `pi-agent-core/src/agent-loop.ts:1970-1974, 1995-1997`; `sdk.ts:4650-4653` is that closure | Low (good) — third param defaults to `\"turn\"`, so main turns stay audited | (c) above |\n| Session keeps its own setting value when `config.yml` gains an unknown key, preserving tools + prompt cache | CHANGELOG `18.18.0` line 66 | Low/none for 0095; relevant to settings-gated families | — |\n| Skill routing hints re-compressed in the system prompt | CHANGELOG `18.18.0` \"Skills listed in the system prompt get their compressed routing hint…\" | None for 0095; one-time prefix byte change on upgrade | belongs to persistent-prefix-resume family |\n\nNo cache-prefix, OAuth or compaction-chain invariant of this capability is touched: the patch only adds a parameter, never reorders messages.\n\n## 5. Worker-thread assumption\n**Answer: NO for the patched code; YES-adjacent for its consumer (silent, not crash).**\n\n- Patch code is per-`AgentSession` state only: `agent-session.ts:1294` field, `:2676` closure over `#transformContext`, `sdk.ts:4650` closure over that session's `extensionRunner` (`sdk.ts:3747` creates one runner per `createAgentSession`, i.e. per thread). No module singleton, timer, global registry or auth store is read or written. Worker-hosted child sessions get their own session object (`task/executor.ts:5033` `createAgentSession`) and therefore their own `\"turn\"`/`\"side-request\"` labelling — behaviour is identical on main and in a worker.\n- The concrete risk with `workerThreads>0` is on the **consumer** side: the audit extension keeps `const lastSignature = new Map<string,string>()` at extension-factory scope (`patches/context-persist-audit-extension.ts:290`, used at `:316`) and appends to one process-wide file `~/.omp/agent/context-persist-audit.log` (`:71`, `:286`) with 1 MiB rotation via `statSync`+`renameSync` (`:285`). Child sessions inherit the prepared extension (`task/executor.ts:4961`, `task/structured-subagent.ts:858`), so subagent `context` events now fire from several threads into the same file. Failure mode: **silent** — interleaved or lost JSONL records (rotation race between threads), never a crash and never extra cost/cache loss; the per-thread `lastSignature` map stays correct (it is per thread, keyed by `sessionId`).\n- Gate leg that would catch it: today **none** — `verify-context-persist-audit.ts:304-374` drives only the main thread with a single session. Required new leg: run the same block with `\"task.workerThreads\": 4` in the `Settings.loadIsolated` overrides (`:330-337`), spawn one `task` subagent through the real executor, then assert (i) no audit record for any `side-request` conversion, (ii) the truncated-main-turn record still appears with the main `sessionId`, (iii) every line in the log parses as JSON and no record is lost across a forced rotation. That leg is the \"PASS\" criterion for turning `task.workerThreads` on at 4.\n\nEffort for the whole item (rebase unchanged + gate version arm + worker leg): **S/M** (worker leg is the only new code; S if the subagent spawn is driven the way family-0101's gate already spawns one).\n"

That leg is the one that catches \"worker-local state read as empty\" (silent cost), which the current single-process run cannot.\n  4. keep hunk #5's cosmetic blank line out.\n\nNon-goals confirmed: capability 0101 is *not* retired; nothing else in the tree depends on its patch lines (single family, single file, `patches/.18.16.0-families/plan.tsv` root AGENT).\n"
